function Hop()
	local placeId = game.PlaceId
	local u1 = {}
	local nextPageCursor = ''
	local hour = os.date('!*t').hour
	function TPReturner()
		local v2
		if nextPageCursor == '' then
			v2 = game.HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet('https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/' .. placeId .. '/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100'))
		else
			v2 = game.HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet('https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/' .. placeId .. '/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100&cursor=' .. nextPageCursor))
		end
		if v2.nextPageCursor and (v2.nextPageCursor ~= 'null' and v2.nextPageCursor ~= 'null') then
			nextPageCursor = v2.nextPageCursor
		end
		local v3, v4, v5 = pairs(v2.data)
		local v6 = 0
		while true do
			local v7
			v5, v7 = v3(v4, v5)
			if v5 == nil then
				break
			end
			local v8 = true
			local u9 = tostring(v7.id)
			if tonumber(v7.maxPlayers) > tonumber(v7.playing) then
				local v10, v11, v12 = pairs(u1)
				while true do
					local v13
					v12, v13 = v10(v11, v12)
					if v12 == nil then
						break
					end
					if v6 == 0 then
						if tonumber(hour) ~= tonumber(v13) then
							pcall(function()
								u1 = {}
								table.insert(u1, hour)
							end)
						end
					elseif u9 == tostring(v13) then
						v8 = false
					end
					v6 = v6 + 1
				end
				if v8 == true then
					table.insert(u1, u9)
					wait(0.1)
					pcall(function()
						wait()
						game:GetService('TeleportService'):TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, u9, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
					end)
					wait(0.1)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	function Teleport()
		while wait(0.1) do
			pcall(function()
				TPReturner()
				if nextPageCursor ~= '' then
					TPReturner()
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	Teleport()
end
function CheckItem(p14)
	local v15, v16, v17 = pairs(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory'))
	while true do
		local v18
		v17, v18 = v15(v16, v17)
		if v17 == nil then
			break
		end
		if v18.Name == p14 then
			return v18
		end
	end
end
function UpdateIslandESP()
	local v19, v20, v21 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace')._WorldOrigin.Locations:GetChildren())
	while true do
		local v22
		v21, v22 = v19(v20, v21)
		if v21 == nil then
			break
		end
		local v222 = v22
		pcall(function()
			if IslandESP then
				if v222.Name ~= 'Sea' then
					if v222:FindFirstChild('NameEsp') then
						v222.NameEsp.TextLabel.Text = v222.Name .. '   \n' .. round((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v222.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
					else
						local billboardGui = Instance.new('BillboardGui', v222)
						billboardGui.Name = 'NameEsp'
						billboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
						billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
						billboardGui.Adornee = v222
						billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
						local textLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel', billboardGui)
						textLabel.Font = 'GothamSemibold'
						textLabel.FontSize = 'Size14'
						textLabel.TextWrapped = true
						textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
						textLabel.TextYAlignment = 'Top'
						textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
						textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					end
				end
			elseif v222:FindFirstChild('NameEsp') then
				v222:FindFirstChild('NameEsp'):Destroy()
			end
		end)
	end
end
function isnil(p23)
	local _ = p23 == nil
	return true
end
local function u25(p24)
	return math.floor(tonumber(p24) + 0.5)
end
Number = math.random(1, 1000000)
function UpdatePlayerChams()
	local v26, v27, v28 = pairs(game:GetService('Players'):GetChildren())
	while true do
		local v29
		v28, v29 = v26(v27, v28)
		if v28 == nil then
			break
		end
		local v292 = v29
		pcall(function()
			if not isnil(v292.Character) then
				if ESPPlayer then
					if isnil(v292.Character.Head) or v292.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
						v292.Character.Head['NameEsp' .. Number].TextLabel.Text = v292.Name .. ' | ' .. u25((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v292.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance\nHealth : ' .. u25(v292.Character.Humanoid.Health * 100 / v292.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth) .. '%'
					else
						local billboardGui = Instance.new('BillboardGui', v292.Character.Head)
						billboardGui.Name = 'NameEsp' .. Number
						billboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
						billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
						billboardGui.Adornee = v292.Character.Head
						billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
						local textLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel', billboardGui)
						textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
						textLabel.FontSize = 'Size14'
						textLabel.TextWrapped = true
						textLabel.Text = v292.Name .. ' \n' .. u25((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v292.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
						textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
						textLabel.TextYAlignment = 'Top'
						textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
						if v292.Team ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer.Team then
							textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(255, 0, 0)
						else
							textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 255, 0)
						end
					end
				elseif v292.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
					v292.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number):Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

function UpdatePlayerChams()
	local v63, v64, v65 = pairs(game:GetService('Players'):GetChildren())
	while true do
		local v66
		v65, v66 = v63(v64, v65)
		if v65 == nil then
			break
		end
		local v662 = v66
		pcall(function()
			if not isnil(v662.Character) then
				if ESPPlayer then
					if isnil(v662.Character.Head) or v662.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
						v662.Character.Head['NameEsp' .. Number].TextLabel.Text = v662.Name .. ' | ' .. u62((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v662.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance\nHealth : ' .. u62(v662.Character.Humanoid.Health * 100 / v662.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth) .. '%'
					else
						local billboardGui = Instance.new('BillboardGui', v662.Character.Head)
						billboardGui.Name = 'NameEsp' .. Number
						billboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
						billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
						billboardGui.Adornee = v662.Character.Head
						billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
						local textLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel', billboardGui)
						textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
						textLabel.FontSize = 'Size14'
						textLabel.TextWrapped = true
						textLabel.Text = v662.Name .. ' \n' .. u62((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v662.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
						textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
						textLabel.TextYAlignment = 'Top'
						textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
						if v662.Team ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer.Team then
							textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(255, 0, 0)
						else
							textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 255, 0)
						end
					end
				elseif v662.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
					v662.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number):Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function u173(p172)
	return math.floor(tonumber(p172) + 0.5)

Number = math.random(1, 1000000)
function UpdatePlayerChams()
	local v174, v175, v176 = pairs(game:GetService('Players'):GetChildren())
	while true do
		local v177
		v176, v177 = v174(v175, v176)
		if v176 == nil then
			break
		end
		local v1772 = v177
		pcall(function()
			if not isnil(v1772.Character) then
				if ESPPlayer then
					if isnil(v1772.Character.Head) or v1772.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
						v1772.Character.Head['NameEsp' .. Number].TextLabel.Text = v1772.Name .. ' | ' .. u173((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v1772.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance\nHealth : ' .. u173(v1772.Character.Humanoid.Health * 100 / v1772.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth) .. '%'
					else
						local billboardGui = Instance.new('BillboardGui', v1772.Character.Head)
						billboardGui.Name = 'NameEsp' .. Number
						billboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
						billboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
						billboardGui.Adornee = v1772.Character.Head
						billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
						local textLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel', billboardGui)
						textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
						textLabel.FontSize = 'Size14'
						textLabel.TextWrapped = true
						textLabel.Text = v1772.Name .. ' \n' .. u173((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v1772.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
						textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
						textLabel.TextYAlignment = 'Top'
						textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
						if v1772.Team == game.Players.LocalPlayer.Team then
							textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 255, 0)
						else
							textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(255, 0, 0)
						end
					end
				elseif v1772.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
					v1772.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number):Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

function InfAb()
	if InfAbility then
		if not game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('Agility') then
			local particleEmitter = Instance.new('ParticleEmitter')
			particleEmitter.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			particleEmitter.Archivable = true
			particleEmitter.Drag = 20
			particleEmitter.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top
			particleEmitter.Enabled = true
			particleEmitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
			particleEmitter.LightInfluence = 0
			particleEmitter.LockedToPart = true
			particleEmitter.Name = 'Agility'
			particleEmitter.Rate = 500
			local v291 = { NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 4) }
			particleEmitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(v291)
			particleEmitter.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(9999, 99999)
			particleEmitter.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
			particleEmitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(30, 30)
			particleEmitter.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			particleEmitter.Texture = ''
			particleEmitter.VelocityInheritance = 0
			particleEmitter.ZOffset = 2
			particleEmitter.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)

		end

		game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('Agility'):Destroy()
	end
end

function AutoHaki()

	local v309 = character and not character:FindFirstChild('HasBuso') and game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_
	if v309 then
		v309:InvokeServer('Buso')
	end
end
function UnEquipWeapon(p310)
	if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(p310) then
		_G.NotAutoEquip = true
		wait(0.5)
		game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(p310).Parent = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack
		wait(0.1)
		_G.NotAutoEquip = false
	end
end
function EquipWeapon(p311)
	if not _G.NotAutoEquip and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(p311) then
		Tool = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(p311)
		wait(0.1)
		game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(Tool)
	end
end
spawn(function()
	local v312 = getrawmetatable(game)
	local namecall = v312.__namecall
	setreadonly(v312, false)
	v312.__namecall = newcclosure(function()
		local v313 = getnamecallmethod()
		local v314 = { ... }
		if tostring(v313) ~= 'FireServer' or (tostring(v314[1]) ~= 'RemoteEvent' or (tostring(v314[2]) == 'true' or (tostring(v314[2]) == 'false' or not _G.UseSkill))) then
			return namecall(...)
		end
		if type(v314[2]) == 'vector' then
			v314[2] = PositionSkillMasteryDevilFruit
		else
			v314[2] = CFrame.new(PositionSkillMasteryDevilFruit)
		end
		return namecall(unpack(v314))
	end)
end)
spawn(function()
	pcall(function()

			local v315, v316, v317 = pairs(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren())
			while true do
				local v318
				v317, v318 = v315(v316, v317)
				if v317 == nil then
					break

				if v318:IsA('Tool') and v318:FindFirstChild('RemoteFunctionShoot') then
					CurrentEquipGun = v318.Name
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end)
function StopTween(p319)
	local character = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character
	if not p319 then
		_G.StopTween = true
		wait(0.2)
		topos(character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
		wait(0.2)
		if character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('BodyClip') then
			character.HumanoidRootPart.BodyClip:Destroy()
		end
		if character:FindFirstChild('Block') then
			character.Block:Destroy()
		end
		_G.StopTween = false
		_G.Clip = false
	end
	if character:FindFirstChild('Highlight') then
		character.Highlight:Destroy()
	end
end
function LockTween()
	if not _G.LockTween then
		_G.LockTween = true
		wait()
		local character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
		local v320 = character and character:IsDescendantOf(game.Workspace) and character:WaitForChild('HumanoidRootPart')
		if v320 then
			v320.CFrame = v320.CFrame
		end
		wait()
		if character:FindFirstChild('BodyClip') then
			character.BodyClip:Destroy()
		end
		if character:FindFirstChild('PartTele') then
			character.Block:Destroy()
		end
		_G.LockTween = false
	end
end
function BringMob(p321)
	local v322, v323, v324 = pairs(WS.Enemies:GetChildren())
	while true do
		local v325
		v324, v325 = v322(v323, v324)
		if v324 == nil then
			break
		end
		if v325.Name == p321 and (v325.Parent and (v325:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (v325:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (v325.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (v325.HumanoidRootPart.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 350)))) then
			v325.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = BringPos
			v325.Humanoid.JumpPower = 0
			v325.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
			v325.HumanoidRootPart.Transparency = 1
			v325.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
			v325.Head.CanCollide = false
			if v325.Humanoid:FindFirstChild('Animator') then
				v325.Humanoid.Animator:Destroy()
			end
			if not v325.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('Lock') then
				local bodyVelocity = Instance.new('BodyVelocity')
				bodyVelocity.Parent = v325.HumanoidRootPart
				bodyVelocity.Name = 'Lock'
				bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(100000, 100000, 100000)
				bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			end
			sethiddenproperty(plr, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
			v325.Humanoid:ChangeState(11)
		end
	end
end
function CancelTween23()
	if plr.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('BodyVelocity') then
		plr.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('BodyVelocity'):Destroy()
	end
	if plr.Character:FindFirstChild('PartTele') then
		plr.Character:FindFirstChild('PartTele'):Destroy()
	end
	NoClip = false
	return Tween23(plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
end
function KillMob(p326, p327)
	pcall(function()
		thismob = DetectMob2(p326)
		if thismob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and thismob.Parent and (thismob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and thismob.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
			repeat
				task.wait()
				Buso()
				EquipWeapon()
				Tween23(thismob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 15, 0))
				BringPos = thismob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
				BringMob(p326)
				NoClip = true
			until not thismob.Parent or (not thismob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') or (thismob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid').Health <= 0 or (not thismob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') or p327())))
			NoClip = false
			CancelTween23()
		end
	end)
end

end)

function enableNoclip()
	if not game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('BodyClip') then
		local bodyVelocity = Instance.new('BodyVelocity')
		bodyVelocity.Name = 'BodyClip'
		bodyVelocity.Parent = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
		bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(100000, 100000, 100000)
		bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	end
end
function disableNoclip()
	local bodyClip = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('BodyClip')
	if bodyClip then
		bodyClip:Destroy()
	end
end
function disableCollisions()
	local v332, v333, v334 = pairs(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants())
	while true do
		local v335
		v334, v335 = v332(v333, v334)
		if v334 == nil then
			break
		end
		if v335:IsA('BasePart') then
			v335.CanCollide = false
		end
	end
end
local _, _ = pcall(function()
	return getgenv().Module
end)

	pcall(function()
		while task.wait(0.2) do
			if getgenv().Module or (_G.DefendVolcano or getgenv().AutoFarm) then
				enableNoclip()
				disableCollisions()
			else
				disableNoclip()
			end
		end
	end)
end)
function EquipAllWeapon()
	pcall(function()
		local v336, v337, v338 = pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren())
		while true do
			local v339
			v338, v339 = v336(v337, v338)
			if v338 == nil then
				break
			end
			if v339:IsA('Tool') and (v339.Name ~= 'Summon Sea Beast' and (v339.Name ~= 'Water Body' and v339.Name ~= 'Awakening')) then
				local v340 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(v339.Name)
				game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v340)
				wait(1)
			end
		end
	end)
end
local u341 = false
function WaitHRP(p342)
	if p342 then
		local waitForChild = p342.Character.WaitForChild
	end
end
function CheckNearestTeleporter(p343)
	local position = p343.Position
	local magnitude2 = math.huge
	local v344 = nil
	local placeId = game.PlaceId
	local v345 = {}
	local v346
	if placeId == 2753915549 then
		v346 = {
			Sky3 = Vector3.new(-7894, 5547, -380),
			Sky3Exit = Vector3.new(-4607, 874, -1667),
			UnderWater = Vector3.new(61163, 11, 1819),
			['Underwater City'] = Vector3.new(61165.19140625, 0.18704631924629211, 1897.379150390625),
			['Pirate Village'] = Vector3.new(-1242.4625244140625, 4.787059783935547, 3901.282958984375),
			UnderwaterExit = Vector3.new(4050, -1, -1814)
		}
	elseif placeId == 4442272183 then
		v346 = {
			['Swan Mansion'] = Vector3.new(-390, 332, 673),
			['Swan Room'] = Vector3.new(2285, 15, 905),
			['Cursed Ship'] = Vector3.new(923, 126, 32852),
			['Zombie Island'] = Vector3.new(-6509, 83, -133)
		}
	else
		v346 = placeId == 7449423635 and {
			['Floating Turtle'] = Vector3.new(-12462, 375, -7552),
			['Hydra Island'] = Vector3.new(5657.88623046875, 1013.0790405273438, -335.4996337890625),
			Mansion = Vector3.new(-12462, 375, -7552),
			Castle = Vector3.new(-5036, 315, -3179),
			['Dimensional Shift'] = Vector3.new(-2097.3447265625, 4776.24462890625, -15013.4990234375),
			['Beautiful Pirate'] = Vector3.new(5319, 23, -93),
			['Beautiful Room'] = Vector3.new(5314.58203, 22.5364361, -125.942276, 1, 2.14762768e-8, -1.99111154e-13, -2.14762768e-8, 1, -3.0510602e-8, 1.98455903e-13, 3.0510602e-8, 1),
			['Temple of Time'] = Vector3.new(28286, 14897, 103)
		} or v345
	end
	local v347, v348, v349 = pairs(v346)
	while true do
		local v350
		v349, v350 = v347(v348, v349)
		if v349 == nil then
			break
		end
		local magnitude = (v350 - position).Magnitude
		if magnitude < magnitude2 then
			v344 = v350
			magnitude2 = magnitude
		end
	end
	if magnitude2 <= (position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude then
		return v344
	end
end
function requestEntrance(p351)
	game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', p351)
	local humanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
	humanoidRootPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
	task.wait(0.5)
end
function TelePPlayer(p352)
	game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p352
end
function topos(p353)
	local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
	if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
		local magnitude = (p353.Position - localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
		if not p353 then
			return
		end
		local v354 = CheckNearestTeleporter(p353)
		if v354 then
			requestEntrance(v354)
		end
		if not localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('PartTele') then
			local part = Instance.new('Part', localPlayer.Character)
			part.Size = Vector3.new(10, 1, 10)
			part.Name = 'PartTele'
			part.Anchored = true
			part.Transparency = 1
			part.CanCollide = true
			part.CFrame = WaitHRP(localPlayer).CFrame
			local part2 = part
			local part22 = part2
			part2.GetPropertyChangedSignal(part22, 'CFrame'):Connect(function()
				if u341 then
					task.wait()
					if localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
						WaitHRP(localPlayer).CFrame = part2.CFrame
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		u341 = true
		local v355 = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(localPlayer.Character.PartTele, TweenInfo.new(magnitude / 360, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
			CFrame = p353
		})
		v355:Play()
		v355.Completed:Connect(function(p356)
			if p356 == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
				if localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('PartTele') then
					localPlayer.Character.PartTele:Destroy()
				end
				u341 = false
			end
		end)
	end
end
function stopTeleport()
	u341 = false
	local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
	if localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('PartTele') then
		localPlayer.Character.PartTele:Destroy()
	end
end
spawn(function()
	while task.wait() do
		if not u341 then
			stopTeleport()
		end
	end
end)
spawn(function()
	local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
	while task.wait() do
		pcall(function()
			if localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('PartTele') and (localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - localPlayer.Character.PartTele.Position).Magnitude >= 100 then
				stopTeleport()
			end
		end)
	end
end)
local localPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local function v358(p357)
	p357:WaitForChild('Humanoid').Died:Connect(function()
		stopTeleport()
	end)
end
localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(v358)
if localPlayer.Character then
	v358(localPlayer.Character)
end

spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		if _G.SpinPos then

local u364 = {}
function TP13(p365)
	local magnitude = (p365.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
	local u366 = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, TweenInfo.new(magnitude / TweenSpeed, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
		CFrame = p365
	}):Play()
	function u364.Stop(_)
		u366:Cancel()
	end
	return u364
end
function fastpos(p367)
	Distance = (p367.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
	Speed = 1000
	game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, TweenInfo.new(Distance / Speed, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
		CFrame = p367
	}):Play()
end
function slowpos(p368)
	Distance = (p368.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
	Speed = 150
	game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, TweenInfo.new(Distance / Speed, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
		CFrame = p368
	}):Play()
end
function BTP(p369)
	pcall(function()
		if (p369.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude >= 1500 and (not Auto_Raid and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
			repeat
				wait()
				game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p369
				wait(0.05)
				game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Head:Destroy()
				game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p369
			until (p369.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < 1500 and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0
		end
	end)
end
function TelePPlayer(p370)
	game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p370
end
function TPB(p371)
	local v372 = game:service('TweenService')
	local v373 = TweenInfo.new((game:GetService('Workspace').Boats.PirateBrigade.VehicleSeat.CFrame.Position - p371.Position).Magnitude / 300, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
	tween = v372:Create(game:GetService('Workspace').Boats.PirateBrigade.VehicleSeat, v373, {
		CFrame = p371
	})
	tween:Play()
	return {
		Stop = function(_)
			tween:Cancel()
		end
	}
end
function TPP(p374)
	if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild('Humanoid').Health > 0 and game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild('Humanoid') then
		local v375 = game:service('TweenService')
		local v376 = TweenInfo.new((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - p374.Position).Magnitude / 325, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
		tween = v375:Create(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, v376, {
			CFrame = p374
		})
		tween:Play()
		return {
			Stop = function(_)
				tween:Cancel()
			end
		}
	end
	tween:Cancel()
	repeat
		wait()
	until game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild('Humanoid') and game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild('Humanoid').Health > 0
	wait(7)
end
function StopTween(p377)
	if not p377 then
		_G.StopTween = true
		wait()
		topos(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
		wait()
		if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('BodyClip') then
			game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('BodyClip'):Destroy()
		end
		_G.StopTween = false
		_G.Clip = false
	end
end

function CheckColorRipIndra()
	mmb = {}
	local next = next
	local v382, v383 = game:GetService('Workspace').Map['Boat Castle'].Summoner.Circle:GetChildren()
	while true do
		local v384
		v383, v384 = next(v382, v383)
		if v383 == nil then
			break
		end
		if v384:IsA('Part') and (v384:FindFirstChild('Part') and v384.Part.BrickColor.Name == 'Dark stone grey') then
			mmb[v384.BrickColor.Name] = v384
		end
	end
	return mmb
end
function ActivateColor(p385)
	haki = {
		['Hot pink'] = 'Winter Sky',
		['Really red'] = 'Pure Red',
		Oyster = 'Snow White'
	}
	runnay = haki[p385]
	if runnay then
		game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('activateColor', runnay)
	end
end
function AutoActiveColorRip_Indra()
	local v386, v387, v388 = pairs(CheckColorRipIndra())
	while true do
		local v389
		v388, v389 = v386(v387, v388)
		if v388 == nil then
			break
		end
		ActivateColor(v388)
		topos(v389.CFrame)
		firetouchinterest(v389.TouchInterest)
	end
end
function CheckRace()
	local v390 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('Wenlocktoad', '1')
	local v391 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('Alchemist', '1')
	if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('RaceTransformed') then
		return game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value .. ' V4'
	elseif v390 == -2 then
		return game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value .. ' V3'
	elseif v391 == -2 then
		return game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value .. ' V2'
	else
		return game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value .. ' V1'
	end
end
_G.TargTrial = 'TargTrial'
function targettrial()
	if _G.TargTrial ~= 'TargTrial' then
		return
	else
		local v392, v393, v394 = pairs(game.Players:GetChildren())
		local c = 450
		local v395 = nil
		while true do
			local v396
			v394, v396 = v392(v393, v394)
			if v394 == nil then
				break
			end
			c = (v396.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
			if c <= c and v396 ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer then
				c = c
				v395 = v396
			end
		end
		if v395 == 'c' then
			return
		elseif _G.TargTrial == 'c' then
			_G.TargTrial = v395
		end
	end
end
function CheckPirateBoat()
	local next = next
	local v397, v398 = game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren()
	local v399 = { 'PirateBrigade', 'PirateBrigade' }
	while true do
		local v400
		v398, v400 = next(v397, v398)
		if v398 == nil then
			break
		end
		if table.find(v399, v400.Name) and (v400:FindFirstChild('Health') and v400.Health.Value > 0) then
			return v400
		end
	end
end

		end
	end
end

	while task.wait() do
		if _G.AutoFarm then
			pcall(function()
				local text = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text
				CheckQuest()
				if not string.find(text, NameMon) then
					StartBring = false
					game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AbandonQuest')
				end
				if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == false then
					StartBring = false
					if BypassTP then
						if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - CFrameQuest.Position).Magnitude > 1500 then
							TP1(CFrameQuest)
						else
							TP1(CFrameQuest)
						end
					else
						TP1(CFrameQuest)
					end
					if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - CFrameQuest.Position).Magnitude <= 20 then
						game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('StartQuest', NameQuest, LevelQuest)
					end
				elseif game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == true then
					if string.find(text, 'kissed') then
						local v455, v456, v457 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren())
						while true do
							local v458
							v457, v458 = v455(v456, v457)
							if v457 == nil then
								break
							end
							if string.find(v458.Name, 'kissed Warrior') then
								if v458:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (v458:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and v458.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
									if string.find(text, NameMon) then
										repeat
											task.wait()
											EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
											PosMon = v458.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
											topos(v458.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
											v458.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
											v458.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
											v458.Head.CanCollide = false
											v458.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(70, 70, 70)
											StartBring = true
											MonFarm = v458.Name
											game:GetService('VirtualUser'):CaptureController()
											game:GetService('VirtualUser'):Button1Down(Vector2.new(1280, 672))

						while true do
							local v462

							if v461 == nil then
								break
							end
							if v462:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (v462:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (v462.Humanoid.Health > 0 and v462.Name == Mon)) then
								if string.find(text, NameMon) then
									repeat
										task.wait()
										EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
										AutoHaki()
										PosMon = v462.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
										topos(v462.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
										v462.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
										v462.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
										v462.Head.CanCollide = false
										v462.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(70, 70, 70)
										StartBring = true
										MonFarm = v462.Name
										game:GetService('VirtualUser'):CaptureController()
										game:GetService('VirtualUser'):Button1Down(Vector2.new(1280, 672))
									until not _G.AutoFarm or (v462.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v462.Parent) or game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == false

function CheckQuestNew()
	local value = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Data.Level.Value
	if 2600 <= value and value <= 2624 then
		MonNew = 'Reef Bandit'
		LevelQuestNew = 1
		NameQuestNew = 'SubmergedQuest1'
		NameMonNew = 'Reef Bandit'
		CFrameQuestNew = CFrame.new(10882.264, -2086.322, 10034.226)
		CFrameMonNew = CFrame.new(10736.6191, -2087.8439, 9338.4882)
	elseif value < 2625 or 2649 < value then
		if 2650 <= value and value <= 2674 then
			MonNew = 'Sea Chanter'
			LevelQuestNew = 1
			NameQuestNew = 'SubmergedQuest2'
			NameMonNew = 'Sea Chanter'
			CFrameQuestNew = CFrame.new(10882.264, -2086.322, 10034.226)
			CFrameMonNew = CFrame.new(10621.0342, -2087.844, 10102.0332)
		elseif 2675 <= value and value <= 2750 then
			MonNew = 'Ocean Prophet'
			LevelQuestNew = 2
			NameQuestNew = 'SubmergedQuest2'
			NameMonNew = 'Ocean Prophet'
			CFrameQuestNew = CFrame.new(10882.264, -2086.322, 10034.226)
			CFrameMonNew = CFrame.new(11056.1445, -2001.6717, 10117.4493)
		end
	else
		MonNew = 'Coral Pirate'
		LevelQuestNew = 2
		NameQuestNew = 'SubmergedQuest1'
		NameMonNew = 'Coral Pirate'
		CFrameQuestNew = CFrame.new(10882.264, -2086.322, 10034.226)
		CFrameMonNew = CFrame.new(10965.1025, -2158.8842, 9177.2597)
	end
end
spawn(function()
	while task.wait() do
		if _G.AutoFarmLevelNew then
			pcall(function()
				local quest = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest
				CheckQuestNew()
				if quest.Visible ~= false then
					local v464, v465, v466 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren())
					while true do
						local v467
						v466, v467 = v464(v465, v466)
						if v466 == nil then
							break
						end
						if v467.Name == MonNew and (v467:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (v467:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and v467.Humanoid.Health > 0)) then
							if string.find(quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text, NameMonNew) then
								repeat
									task.wait()
									EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
									AutoHaki()
									topos(v467.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
									v467.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
									v467.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
									v467.Head.CanCollide = false
									v467.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(70, 70, 70)
									StartBring = true
									MonFarmNew = v467.Name
									game:GetService('VirtualUser'):CaptureController()
									game:GetService('VirtualUser'):Button1Down(Vector2.new(1280, 672))
								until not _G.AutoFarmLevelNew or (v467.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or (not v467.Parent or quest.Visible == false))
							else
								StartBring = false
								game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AbandonQuest')
							end
						end
					end
					if not game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:FindFirstChild(MonNew) then
						TP1(CFrameMonNew)
						StartBring = false
					end
				else
					StartBring = false
					if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - CFrameQuestNew.Position).Magnitude <= 20 then
						game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('StartQuest', NameQuestNew, LevelQuestNew)
					else
						TP1(CFrameQuestNew)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

function TweenToPosition(p511)
	local humanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
	if humanoidRootPart then
		humanoidRootPart = humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
	end
	if humanoidRootPart then
		local tweenService = game:GetService('TweenService')
		local v512 = (humanoidRootPart.Position - p511.Position).Magnitude / 300
		local v513 = tweenService:Create(humanoidRootPart, TweenInfo.new(v512, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
			CFrame = p511
		})
		v513:Play()
		v513.Completed:Wait()
	end
end
function Skill(p514)
	local virtualInputManager = game:GetService('VirtualInputManager')
	virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode[p514], false, game)
	task.wait(0.05)
	virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode[p514], false, game)
end
function Click()
	local virtualInputManager = game:GetService('VirtualInputManager')
	virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
	task.wait(0.05)
	virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
end
function FindWeapon(p515)
	local backpack = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack
	local v516, v517, v518 = ipairs(backpack:GetChildren())
	while true do
		local v519
		v518, v519 = v516(v517, v518)
		if v518 == nil then
			break
		end
		if v519:IsA('Tool') then
			if p515 == 'Melee' and (v519.ToolTip == 'Melee' or v519.Name == 'Combat') then
				return v519.Name
			end
			if p515 == 'Sword' and v519.ToolTip == 'Sword' then
				return v519.Name
			end
			if p515 == 'Gun' and v519.ToolTip == 'Gun' then
				return v519.Name
			end
			if p515 == 'Fruit' and v519.ToolTip == 'Blox Fruit' then
				return v519.Name
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end
function EquipWeapon(p520)
	if p520 then
		local localPlayer2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
		local v521 = localPlayer2:WaitForChild('Backpack'):FindFirstChild(p520)
		if v521 then
			localPlayer2.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v521)
		end
	end
end
function AttackAllSkills()
	local v522 = FindWeapon('Melee')
	local v523 = FindWeapon('Sword')
	local v524 = FindWeapon('Fruit')
	local v525 = FindWeapon('Gun')
	if v522 then
		EquipWeapon(v522)
		Skill('Z')
		Skill('X')
		Skill('C')
		Skill('V')
		Click()
	end
	if v523 then
		EquipWeapon(v523)
		Skill('Z')
		Skill('X')
		Click()
	end
	if v524 then
		EquipWeapon(v524)
		Skill('Z')
		Skill('X')
		Skill('C')
		Skill('F')
		Click()
	end
	if v525 then
		EquipWeapon(v525)
		Skill('Z')
		Skill('X')
		Click()
	end
end

										sethiddenproperty(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
									until _G.Fullykatakuri == false or (v580.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v580.Parent) or (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild('God\'s Chalice') or game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('God\'s Chalice'))
								end
							end
						elseif game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):FindFirstChild('Diablo') then
							topos(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):FindFirstChild('Diablo').HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(2, 20, 2))

						end
					end

					wait(0.5)
					game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('EliteHunter')
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		if _G.CollectBerry then
			local localPlayer2 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
			local position = (localPlayer2.Character or localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()):GetPivot().Position
			local v582 = game:GetService('CollectionService'):GetTagged('BerryBush')
			local magnitude2 = math.huge
			local v583, v584, v585 = ipairs(v582)
			local v586 = nil
			local v587 = nil
			while true do
				local v588
				v585, v588 = v583(v584, v585)
				if v585 == nil then
					break
				end
				local v589, v590, v591 = pairs(v588:GetAttributes())
				while true do
					local v592
					v591, v592 = v589(v590, v591)
					if v591 == nil then
						break
					end
					local magnitude = (v588.Parent:GetPivot().Position - position).Magnitude
					if magnitude < magnitude2 then
						v587 = v591
						v586 = v588
						magnitude2 = magnitude
					end
				end
			end
			if v586 and v587 then
				local parent = v586.Parent
				local position2 = parent:GetPivot().Position
				TP1(CFrame.new(position2 + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)))
				task.wait(0.5)
				local v593 = parent:FindFirstChild(v587)
				if v593 and v593:IsA('BasePart') then
					TP1(v593.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
					task.wait(0.3)
					local virtualInputManager = game:GetService('VirtualInputManager')
					virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
					task.wait(0.1)
					virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
				end
			elseif _G.CollectBerryHop then
				Hop()
			end
		end
	end
end)

		MaterialPos = CFrame.new(-1211.87, 4.78, 3916.83)

local workspace = workspace
local localPlayer2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local fishReplicated = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild('FishReplicated')
local fishingRequest = fishReplicated:WaitForChild('FishingRequest')
local maxLaunchDistance = require(fishReplicated.FishingClient.Config).Rod.MaxLaunchDistance
local u621 = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Util.GetWaterHeightAtLocation)
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait() do
		if _G.AutoFishing then
			local character = localPlayer2.Character
			local character2
			if character then
				character2 = character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
			else
				character2 = character
			end
			local character3
			if character then
				character3 = character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
			else
				character3 = character
			end
			local character33
			if _G.SelectedRod and (not character3 or character3.Name ~= _G.SelectedRod) then
				character33 = localPlayer2.Backpack:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectedRod)
				if character33 then
					localPlayer2.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(character33)
				else
					character33 = character3
				end
			else
				character33 = character3
			end
			if character and (character2 and character33) then
				local v622 = u621(character2.Position)
				local _, v623 = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(Ray.new(character.Head.Position, character2.CFrame.LookVector * maxLaunchDistance), { character, workspace.Characters, workspace.Enemies })
				if v623 then
					v623 = Vector3.new(v623.X, math.max(v623.Y, v622), v623.Z)
				end
				local v624 = character33:GetAttribute('State')
				local v625 = character33:GetAttribute('ServerState')
				if (v624 == 'ReeledIn' or v625 == 'ReeledIn') and v623 then
					fishingRequest:InvokeServer('StartCasting')
					task.wait()
					fishingRequest:InvokeServer('CastLineAtLocation', v623, 100, true)
				elseif v625 == 'Biting' then
					fishingRequest:InvokeServer('Catching', true)
					task.wait(0.1)
					fishingRequest:InvokeServer('Catch', 1)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

function checkQuesta()
	local u779 = {
		{
			Context = 'Check'
		}
	}
	local u780 = nil
	pcall(function()
		local v781 = {
			{
				Context = 'RequestQuest'
			}
		}
		game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Modules.Net['RF/DragonHunter']:InvokeServer(unpack(v781))
	end)
	local _, _ = pcall(function()
		u780 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Modules.Net['RF/DragonHunter']:InvokeServer(unpack(u779))
	end)
	local v782 = nil
	local v783 = nil
	local v784 = nil
	local v785, v786
	if u780 and u780.Text then
		v785 = true
		local text = u780.Text
		if string.find(text, 'Defeat') then
			v783 = tonumber(string.sub(text, 8, 9))
			local v787, v788, v789 = pairs({ 'Hydra Enforcer', 'Venomous Assailant' })
			v784 = 1
			while true do
				v789, v786 = v787(v788, v789)
				if v789 == nil then
					v786 = v782
					break
				end
				if string.find(text, v786) then
					break
				end
			end
		elseif string.find(text, 'Destroy') then
			v786 = v782
			v783 = 10
			v784 = 2
		else
			v786 = v782
		end
	else
		v786 = v782
		v785 = false
	end
	return v785, v786, v783, v784
end
function BackTODoJo()
	local v790, v791, v792 = pairs(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Notifications:GetChildren())
	while true do
		local v793
		v792, v793 = v790(v791, v792)
		if v792 == nil then
			break
		end
		if v793.Name == 'NotificationTemplate' and string.find(v793.Text, 'Head back to the Dojo to complete more tasks') then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end
function DragonMobClear(p794, p795, p796)
	if workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild(p795) then
		local v797, v798, v799 = pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren())
		while true do
			local v800
			v799, v800 = v797(v798, v799)
			if v799 == nil then
				break
			end
			if v800.Name == p795 and (Attack.Alive(v800) and p794) then
				Attack.Kill(v800, p794)
			end
		end
	elseif p796 then
		topos(p796)
	end
end

local function u859()
	local interiorLava = game.Workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core:FindFirstChild('InteriorLava')
	if interiorLava and interiorLava:IsA('Model') then
		interiorLava:Destroy()
	end
	local prehistoricIsland = game.Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('PrehistoricIsland')
	if prehistoricIsland then
		local v847, v848, v849 = pairs(prehistoricIsland:GetDescendants())
		while true do
			local v850
			v849, v850 = v847(v848, v849)
			if v849 == nil then
				break
			end
			if v850:IsA('Part') and v850.Name:lower():find('lava') then
				v850:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end
	if prehistoricIsland then
		local v851, v852, v853 = pairs(prehistoricIsland:GetDescendants())
		while true do
			local v854
			v853, v854 = v851(v852, v853)
			if v853 == nil then
				break
			end
			if v854:IsA('Model') then
				local v855, v856, v857 = pairs(v854:GetDescendants())
				while true do
					local v858
					v857, v858 = v855(v856, v857)
					if v857 == nil then
						break
					end
					if v858:IsA('MeshPart') and v858.Name:lower():find('lava') then
						v858:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
local function u864()
	local volcanoRocks = game.Workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.VolcanoRocks
	local v860, v861, v862 = pairs(volcanoRocks:GetChildren())
	while true do
		local v863
		v862, v863 = v860(v861, v862)
		if v862 == nil then
			break
		end
		if v863:IsA('Model') then
			local volcanorock = v863:FindFirstChild('volcanorock')
			if volcanorock and volcanorock:IsA('MeshPart') then
				local color = volcanorock.Color
				if color == Color3.fromRGB(185, 53, 56) or color == Color3.fromRGB(185, 53, 57) then
					return volcanorock
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

							task.wait()
							AutoHaki()
							EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)

							v1019.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0

						until not _G.AutoKillV4 or (v1019.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not (v1019.Parent and (v1019:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and v1019:FindFirstChild('Humanoid'))))
					end

		end
	end

local function u1037(p1028)
	if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Island ' .. p1028) then
		local v1029, v1030, v1031 = pairs(workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:GetChildren())
		local magnitude = 4500
		while true do
			local v1032
			v1031, v1032 = v1029(v1030, v1031)
			if v1031 == nil then
				break
			end
			if v1032.Name == 'Island ' .. p1028 and (v1032.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < magnitude then
				magnitude = (v1032.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
			end
		end
		local v1033, v1034, v1035 = pairs(workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:GetChildren())
		while true do
			local v1036
			v1035, v1036 = v1033(v1034, v1035)
			if v1035 == nil then
				break
			end
			if v1036.Name == 'Island ' .. p1028 and (v1036.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= magnitude then
				return v1036
			end
		end
	end
end
local function u1042()
	local v1038, v1039, v1040 = pairs({
		5,
		4,
		3,
		2,
		1
	})
	while true do
		local v1041
		v1040, v1041 = v1038(v1039, v1040)
		if v1040 == nil then
			break
		end
		if u1037(v1041) and (u1037(v1041).Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 4500 then
			return u1037(v1041)
		end
	end
end
local function u1052()
	local v1043, v1044, v1045 = pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren())
	local v1046 = {}
	while true do
		local v1047
		v1045, v1047 = v1043(v1044, v1045)
		if v1045 == nil then
			break
		end
		if v1047:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (v1047:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (v1047.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (v1047.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 1000)) then
			table.insert(v1046, v1047)
		end
	end
	local v1048, v1049, v1050 = pairs(v1046)
	::l13::
	if false then
		return
	end
	local v1051
	v1050, v1051 = v1048(v1049, v1050)
	if v1050 == nil then
		break
	end
	while true do
		task.wait(0.1)
		if v1051:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and v1051.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
			EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
			topos(v1051.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
		end
		if not v1051:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') or v1051.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			goto l13
		end
	end
end
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait() do
		if _G.Dungeon then
			u1052()
			if u1042() then
				topos(u1042().CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 60, 0))
			end
		end
	end
end)

	local v1080 = tostring(p1079)
	repeat
		local v1081

	until v1081 == 0
	return v1080
end

end)

	while wait() do

			if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible ~= false then
				local v1115, v1116, v1117 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace').Characters:GetChildren())
				while true do
					local v1118
					v1117, v1118 = v1115(v1116, v1117)
					if v1117 == nil then
						break
					end

						repeat

							AutoHaki()

							Useskill = true
							topos(v1118.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(1, 7, 3))
							v1118.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(60, 60, 60)
							game:GetService('VirtualUser'):CaptureController()
							game:GetService('VirtualUser'):Button1Down(Vector2.new(1280, 672))

						Useskill = false

					end
				end
			else
				wait(0.5)

		end
	end
end)

v436:AddToggle({
	Name = 'Bring Mod',
	Description = 'T\225\187\177 \196\144\225\187\153ng Gom Qu\195\161i',
	Default = true,
	Callback = function(p1138)
		_G.BringMonster = p1138
		StopTween(_G.BringMonster)
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while task.wait() do
		pcall(function()
			CheckQuest()
			local v1139, v1140, v1141 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren())
			while true do
				local v1142
				v1141, v1142 = v1139(v1140, v1141)
				if v1141 == nil then
					break
				end
				if _G.BringMonster and (StartBring and v1142.Name == MonFarm or v1142.Name == Mon and (v1142:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (v1142:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and v1142.Humanoid.Health > 0)) and (v1142.HumanoidRootPart.Position - game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 320) then
					if v1142.Name ~= 'Factory Staff' then
						if (v1142.Name == MonFarm or v1142.Name == Mon) and (v1142.HumanoidRootPart.Position - PosMon.Position).Magnitude <= 320 then
							v1142.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(60, 60, 60)
							v1142.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = PosMon
							v1142.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
							v1142.Head.CanCollide = false
							if v1142.Humanoid:FindFirstChild('Animator') then
								v1142.Humanoid.Animator:Destroy()
							end
							sethiddenproperty(game.Players.LocalPlayer, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
						end
					elseif (v1142.HumanoidRootPart.Position - PosMon.Position).Magnitude <= 250 then
						v1142.Head.CanCollide = false
						v1142.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
						v1142.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(60, 60, 60)
						v1142.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = PosMon
						if v1142.Humanoid:FindFirstChild('Animator') then
							v1142.Humanoid.Animator:Destroy()
						end
						sethiddenproperty(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)
function InMyNetWork(p1143)
	if isnetworkowner then
		return isnetworkowner(p1143)
	else
		return (p1143.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 320
	end
end

	end
})
spawn(function()
	while task.wait(1) do
		if InfiniteSoru and game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') ~= 'HumanoidRootPart' then
			pcall(function()
				local next = next
				local v1149, v1150 = getgc()
				while true do
					local v1151
					v1150, v1151 = next(v1149, v1150)
					if v1150 == nil then
						break
					end
					if getfenv(v1151).script == game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild('Soru') then
						local v1152, v1153, v1154 = pairs(debug.getupvalues(v1151))
						while true do
							local v1155
							v1154, v1155 = v1152(v1153, v1154)
							if v1154 == nil then
								break
							end
							if type(v1155) == 'table' and v1155.LastUse then
								local v11542 = v1154
								repeat
									task.wait(0.1)

function NoCooldown()
	local next = next
	local v1157, v1158 = getgc()
	while true do
		local v1159
		v1158, v1159 = next(v1157, v1158)
		if v1158 == nil then
			break
		end
		if typeof(v1159) == 'function' and getfenv(v1159).script == game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild('Dodge') then
			local next2 = next
			local v1160, v1161 = getupvalues(v1159)
			while true do
				local v1162
				v1161, v1162 = next2(v1160, v1161)
				if v1161 == nil then
					break
				end
				if tostring(v1162) == '0.4' then
					setupvalue(v1159, v1161, 0)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		if DodgewithoutCool then
			pcall(function()
				NoCooldown()
			end)
		end
	end
end)

			local v1189
			v1187, v1189 = v1185(v1186, v1187)
			if v1187 == nil then
				break
			end
			if v1189:IsA('Part') or (v1189:IsA('Union') or (v1189:IsA('CornerWedgePart') or v1189:IsA('TrussPart'))) then
				v1189.Material = 'Plastic'
				v1189.Reflectance = 0
			elseif v1189:IsA('Decal') or v1189:IsA('Texture') and v1188 then
				v1189.Transparency = 1
			elseif v1189:IsA('ParticleEmitter') or v1189:IsA('Trail') then
				v1189.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
			elseif v1189:IsA('Explosion') then
				v1189.BlastPressure = 1
				v1189.BlastRadius = 1
			elseif v1189:IsA('Fire') or (v1189:IsA('SpotLight') or v1189:IsA('Smoke')) then
				v1189.Enabled = false
			end
		end
	end
})
